# @openfolia/api

Content API over a content repository (see `@openfolia/content-repo` for the layout). NestJS 12 on Express.

```sh
cp .env.example .env   # set CCMS_CONTENT_DIR and CCMS_DATABASE_URL (CCMS_API_TOKEN only for local use)
pnpm nx build api && node --env-file=.env backend/api/dist/main.js
```

**Local testing** with a sample content repository (bare, branches `main` and `draft`, tag `v1`) and Postgres in Docker:

```sh
docker compose up -d          # Postgres on 127.0.0.1:5432 and S3 (SeaweedFS) on 127.0.0.1:8333 (compose.yaml at the repository root)
pnpm nx run api:setup-local   # creates .local/sample-content.git, .env.local (random token, demo mode), the database and the bucket; seeds the example users; prints the token
pnpm nx run api:serve-local   # builds and starts the API with .env.local
```

Both files are git-ignored and kept on later runs (a setting added since is appended to `.env.local`, and an empty `CCMS_AUTH_MODE=` is filled in; a value you set is kept). A local run starts in demo mode (`CCMS_AUTH_MODE=demo`): with Postgres up, `setup-local` seeds the example roles and users (`db-seed --example`) into a database that has no users yet, so the editor's sign-in page lists them; `node backend/api/scripts/setup-local.ts --force` recreates them. To try real content, point `CCMS_CONTENT_DIR` in `.env.local` at a bare clone of the content repository (`git clone --bare`): the API writes branches to it (change sets), and `GitWriter` never moves a branch checked out in a working tree. A `.env.local` still naming the old `.local/sample-content` (a sample with a working tree) is moved to the bare one by the next `setup-local`. Tests never need Docker (see "Database").

**API reference:** `GET /docs` (interactive), `GET /openapi.json` or `GET /openapi.yaml`. The document is generated from the controllers and the Zod schemas of the resources, so it always matches the server; the end-to-end tests check every JSON response against those schemas.

**Delivery API** (#174): a second document with only what someone building their own viewer, portal or integration needs: `GET /docs/delivery` (interactive), `GET /openapi/delivery.json` and `.yaml`, with its own introduction (authentication (the intended consumer token acts through a role holding only `content.read`), refs and caching, variants and languages, images, HTML and PDF, errors; `base/api/delivery-intro.ts`). A route is part of it only when its controller method carries `@DeliveryApi()` (`x-audience: delivery`): an explicit opt-in, never derived from the permission, so a new read route a consumer needs gets the decorator and nothing else is shown. The document is generated from the same controllers and schemas, keeps only the marked operations and the schemas they reference, and leaves out the demo-mode header. `tests/delivery-api.e2e.spec.ts` fails for a delivery route that is not `GET`/`HEAD` or needs more than `content.read` (or no permission), for a schema that no kept operation uses and for a reference that does not resolve. `pnpm nx run api:openapi-delivery` writes the YAML to `openapi/delivery.yaml` (committed, for publishing and diffs); `api:check-openapi-delivery`, part of `lint` and so of `pnpm check`, fails when it is stale. Like the full document it is OpenAPI 3.0 (what the Zod schemas convert to), not 3.1. `GET /releases` and `GET /releases/:releaseId` are part of it (#40), and `GET /releases/:releaseId/changes` and `GET /changelog` (#138), `GET /releases/:releaseId/outputs` and its files (#249), and the iiRDS packages (#431); downloads join with `@DeliveryApi()` when they exist.

Every request needs `Authorization: Bearer <token>`, except `GET /health`, `GET /about` and the API reference (see "Service accounts and tokens"). The token is a **service account's** (`ccms_sat_…`), acting with that account's roles, or, only when `CCMS_API_TOKEN` is set (local use and demo mode; unset by default, and the server warns when it is set outside demo mode), the **shared token**, which acts as a read-only service (actor `service:api-token`, an ID no user can have: `content.read` and `content.read.draft`, everywhere). In demo mode (`CCMS_AUTH_MODE=demo`, off by default; the server warns at start-up; it requires `CCMS_API_TOKEN`) the shared token is still required, and `X-Ccms-User: <user ID>` picks a seeded user to act as; one that names no user is 403 `unknown-user` (the token was valid, so no 401). Without demo mode that header is refused (400 `demo-mode-off`), with a service account's token always (400 `user-header-not-accepted`), and it never authenticates on its own (no valid token: 401). See "Users, roles and permissions".

**JSON:API.** JSON responses follow [JSON:API](https://jsonapi.org/format/) with the media type `application/vnd.api+json` (exactly; `Accept` must allow it, or the answer is 406):

- Resources are `{ type, id, attributes, relationships?, meta? }`. `attributes` hold stored data, in camelCase; `meta` holds what the server derives from it (validation issues, variant summaries). JSON:API reserves `id` and `type`, so a module's own ID and type are `moduleId` and `moduleType`, and a manifest's `attributes` are `textAttributes`.
- Every list is paged: `page[number]` (from 1) and `page[size]` (default 50, at most 200), with `meta.page` (`currentPage`, `lastPage`, `perPage`, `total`) and `links` (`self`, `first`, `last`, `prev`, `next`). Filters are `filter[<name>]`. A query parameter a route does not support is a 400.
- Errors, on every route including binary ones: `{ "errors": [{ "status", "title", "detail", "code", "source"?, "meta"? }] }`. `code` is stable (`not-found`, `invalid-content`, `invalid-parameter`, `renderer-busy`, …); `source.parameter` names a bad parameter; `meta.path` is the broken repository file, `meta.retryAfter` the seconds to wait.

**Cross-site requests** (CSRF, #202). Every request that may change state (any method but `GET`, `HEAD` and `OPTIONS`), on every route, must carry what a cross-site HTML form, or a `fetch` that skips the CORS preflight, cannot send: `Content-Type: application/vnd.api+json`, **also without a body** (a body-less transition, a `DELETE`). Without a `Content-Type` that is 415 `content-type-required`; with another one 415 `unsupported-media-type`. A route that takes another body type (`@AcceptsBodyOf`; today only the image upload, `multipart/form-data`, which a form can send) also needs `X-Ccms-Request` (any non-empty value) with a body of that type, else 403 `request-header-required`. `CrossSiteRequestGuard` (`base/api/cross-site.guard.ts`) is a global guard (`APP_GUARD` in `AppModule`), checked before the token: **a new route is covered without opting in**, and `tests/cross-site.e2e.spec.ts` walks every state-changing route the app serves. The API itself sends no CORS headers, so a browser on another origin cannot get past its preflight. The editor's dev server does send them (`Access-Control-Allow-Origin`) on what it proxies, so what adds the credentials in front of the API (the dev proxy, a deployed reverse proxy) must check `Origin` against the editor's own; this is required, not a second layer (`frontend/editor/README.md`, "Local development and the token").

Every content route accepts `?ref=` (branch, tag or commit ID; default `CCMS_DEFAULT_REF`; refs other than `main` and tags need `content.read.draft`) and returns the commit it read in `X-Ccms-Commit`. Responses requested by commit ID are marked immutable (except the module routes, whose status depends on more: see "Changes and status"); others revalidate with `ETag`. A read at the tag of a withdrawn release (by name, or as `refs/tags/<tag>`; a branch of the same name wins, as for reading) keeps working and says so in `X-Ccms-Withdrawn: true` (#330), and its ETag changes with the withdrawal. What a route returns is limited to the reader's scope (see "Reading scope").

**Names from before the product was named OpenFolia** stay where they are part of the API's contract or of stored data: the `X-Ccms-*` headers, the settings' `CCMS_` prefix, the token prefixes (`ccms_sat_`, `ccms_ses_`, `ccms_pwl_`, `ccms_ecl_`), the XLIFF namespace (`urn:ccms:xliff:1.0`), the configuration's stored subject ID (`ccms.yaml`, in locks, working copies and the audit log), key derivation contexts, advisory lock names, the secret key file (`ccms-secret.key`) and the database names. A content repository's configuration is `openfolia.yaml`; `ccms.yaml` is still read for one release (`@openfolia/content-model`'s README, "Config").

| Route                                                                          | Returns                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| ---------------------------------------------------------                      | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `GET /about`                                                                   | the licence and where the source is (no token; see "Licence and source")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `GET /health`                                                                  | liveness (no token, plain JSON): the server, its database and pool                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `GET /health/ready`                                                            | readiness (no token): 503 when the database is unusable                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `GET /refs`                                                                    | `Ref` resources the reader may open: branches, then tags, with commits; a tag's `meta.withdrawal` (`{ by, at, reason }`, null while it stands)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `GET /config`                                                                  | the `ProjectConfig` resource                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `GET /config/usages`                                                           | `ConfigUsage` resources: per configured value, how many modules (by ID) and manuals name it, as the reader sees them (project settings)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `GET /variants`                                                                | `VariantAttribute` resources: the configured variant attributes with their values (see "Variants")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `GET /variants/matches?when=`                                                  | `VariantBuild`s: the manuals' listed variant combinations a condition holds for (see "Variants")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `GET /variants/:attributeId/usages?filter[value]=`                             | `VariantUse`s: the manuals and modules naming a variant attribute or one of its values, from the module index (see "Variants")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `GET /modules?filter[…]=&sort=`                                                | `Module` resources with `meta.status` and where each is used (`meta.usedIn`, `meta.usage`), filtered and sorted on the module index (see "Module index"); unreadable files with `meta.error`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `GET /modules/:lang/:id`                                                       | a `Module` with its validation issues and status in `meta`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `GET /modules/:lang/:id/history`                                               | `ModuleVersion`s: the commits on `?ref=`’s first-parent line that changed the module, newest first (see "Module history")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `GET /modules/:lang/:id/compare?base=&head=`                                   | a `ModuleComparison`: the module at two versions and the blocks that differ (see "Module history")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `GET /module-summaries?filter[…]=`                                             | `ModuleSummary`s: one per module ID, with languages, filtered (`lang`, `type`, `audience`, `facet`, `keyword`, `changed`, `q`, `snippet`, `deprecated`, `id`); `meta.changed` against `main`, `meta.text`. `filter[status]=deleted` (or `any`, both in one list): the modules deleted on the ref's first-parent line (newest 1,000 commits) that the reader sees no file of now, from the last versions they may see (`meta.deleted`, #320); outside their scope never listed or counted |
| `GET /manuals`                                                                 | `Manual` resources; unreadable manifests are listed with `meta.error`; `filter[status]`: deleted ones (see "Manual history")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `GET /manuals/:id`                                                             | a `Manual`: the manifest, with variant slugs and labels and issues in `meta`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `GET /manuals/:id/checks`                                                      | `Check`s: blocking issues and warnings in a manual (see "Checks")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `GET /manuals/:id/history`                                                     | `ManualVersion`s: the commits that changed the manual's manifest, newest first (see "Manual history")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `GET /manuals/:id/compare?base=&head=`                                         | a `ManualComparison`: the manual at two versions and the members that differ (see "Manual history")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `GET /manuals/:id/variants/:variant/:lang`                                     | a `ResolvedManual`, related to its `Manual`, with issues in `meta`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `GET /manuals/:id/variants/:variant/:lang/pdf`                                 | the PDF (`X-Ccms-Issues` gives the issue count)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `GET /modules/:lang/:id/preview?variant=`                                      | a module as an HTML page (`text/html`; see "HTML previews")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `GET /manuals/:id/variants/:variant/:lang/html`                                | a manual as an HTML page (see "HTML previews")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `GET /modules/:lang/:id/hidden-content`                                        | `HiddenContent`s: what the module's variant blocks leave out for `?variant=` (see "HTML previews")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `GET /manuals/:id/variants/:variant/:lang/hidden-content`                      | `HiddenContent`s: what the manual's conditions leave out for the variant (see "HTML previews")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `GET /preview/:name`, `/preview/fonts/:name`                                   | the stylesheets and fonts the previews link (any actor, no permission)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `GET /search?filter[q]=`                                                       | `SearchResult`s: modules by title, keywords and text, manuals by title, terms by variants and definition; module filters (see "Search")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `GET /terms?filter[…]=`                                                        | `Term` resources by ID, filtered (`lang`, `status`, `domain`, `q`); unreadable term files listed with `meta.error` when unfiltered (see "Terms")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `GET /terms/:termId`                                                           | a `Term` with its validation issues and the modules linking it in `meta` (see "Terms")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `GET /terms/export?format=tbx\|csv&lang=`                                      | the terms as a TBX-Basic or CSV download (see "Terms")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `GET /chunks?filter[…]=`                                                       | `Chunk`s: retrieval chunks, one per module and language, as Markdown with their metadata and hashes (see "Chunks")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `GET /symbols/:id`                                                             | a safety symbol's artwork (`symbols/<id>.svg` or `.png`) at `?ref=`, served as an image that runs nothing (see "Safety symbols")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `GET /assets?filter[…]=`                                                       | `Asset` resources: the images and attachments readable modules use, with type, size, dimensions, file name and `meta.usedIn` (see "Image list")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `GET /assets/:sha256`                                                          | an image or an attached file, streamed from the blob store (see "Blob store" and "Attachments"); `?width=` a WebP rendition (see "Media library")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `DELETE /assets/:sha256`                                                       | deletes a file nobody uses, with its renditions (`content.edit`, unrestricted scope; see "Media library")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `GET /importers`                                                               | `Importer`s, paged, each with its options as JSON Schema in `meta.optionsSchema` and the largest upload in `meta.maxBytes` (`admin.roles`; see "Imports")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `POST /imports`                                                                | checks an upload (multipart): a dry run (200, nothing stored) or stored for an hour to apply (201), the report in `meta.report` (`admin.roles` and `content.edit`; see "Imports")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `GET /imports/:importId`                                                       | an `Import` you uploaded: its report, state, progress and change set (`admin.roles`; see "Imports")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `POST /imports/:importId/apply`                                                | applies a validated import into a new change set in a background job (202; `meta.skipRefused` leaves its refused items out; `admin.roles` and `content.edit`; see "Imports")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `GET /audit-events`                                                            | `AuditEvent` resources, newest first (`admin.roles`; see "Audit log")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `GET /audit-events/export?format=csv\|jsonl`                                   | the same events as a streamed file, CSV or JSON Lines (`admin.roles`; recorded as `audit-log.export`; see "Audit log")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `GET /me`                                                                      | the `Actor` this request acts as: name, roles, effective permissions in meta                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `GET /users`                                                                   | `User` resources with their roles (relationships); needs `admin.roles`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `GET`, `POST /users`; `GET`, `PATCH /users/:userId`                            | read, add or change users and their roles (`admin.roles`; see "Administering access")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `POST`, `DELETE /users/:userId/relationships/roles`                            | gives a user roles, or takes them (`admin.roles`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `POST /users/:userId/deactivate`, `…/reactivate`                               | a deactivated user cannot act; its locks are released (`admin.roles`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `GET`, `POST /service-accounts`; `GET`, `PATCH …/:serviceAccountId`            | service accounts with their roles; `…/relationships/roles`, `…/deactivate`, `…/reactivate` as for users (`admin.roles`; see "Service accounts and tokens")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `GET`, `POST /service-accounts/:serviceAccountId/tokens`                       | its tokens (never the token), or a new one, shown once in `meta.token` (`admin.roles`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `POST /service-accounts/:serviceAccountId/tokens/:tokenId/revoke`              | revokes a token, refused from the next request on (`admin.roles`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `GET`, `POST /roles`; `GET`, `PATCH`, `DELETE /roles/:roleId`                  | `Role` resources: read, create, change, delete (`admin.roles`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `GET /demo/users`                                                              | the same, for a demo client's user switcher: demo mode only, else 404                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `GET /change-sets`                                                             | `ChangeSet` resources, newest first, `meta.counts` per state (`content.read.draft`; see "Change sets")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `POST /change-sets`                                                            | starts a change set on its own branch (`content.edit`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `GET /change-sets/:changeSetId`                                                | a `ChangeSet` (`content.read.draft`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `GET /change-sets/:changeSetId/changes`                                        | `ChangeSetChange`s: what it changes, block by block (see "Changes and status")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `POST /change-sets/:changeSetId/abandon`                                       | abandons an open change set (`content.edit`, its author only)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `POST /change-sets/:changeSetId/assets`                                        | uploads an image or (`?kind=attachment`) an attachment into a draft (`content.edit`; see "Image uploads", "Attachments")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `GET /change-sets/:changeSetId/assets/:hash`                                   | a file uploaded into it, for its author's preview (`content.read`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `POST /change-sets/:changeSetId/locks`                                         | locks a module or manual for the change set (`content.edit`; see "Locks")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `GET /locks`                                                                   | `Lock` resources: who holds what, newest first (`content.read.draft`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `GET /locks/:lockId`                                                           | a `Lock` (`content.read.draft`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `DELETE /locks/:lockId`                                                        | releases a lock (`content.edit`, its holder only); `?ifUnchanged=true` only while its change set has no changes to the subject                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `POST /locks/:lockId/break`                                                    | breaks someone's lock, with a required comment (`admin.roles`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `POST /locks/break`                                                            | breaks all locks of a change set or a holder, with a count (`admin.roles`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `PUT`, `GET`, `DELETE` `…/working-copy`                                        | a module's, manual's, the configuration's (`…/config/working-copy`) or a term's (`…/terms/:termId/working-copy`) working copy (see "Working copies")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `POST /change-sets/:changeSetId/commits`                                       | a save point: commits working copies to the branch (see "Save points")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `POST /change-sets/:changeSetId/submit`                                        | submits a draft for review (`content.edit`; see "Review workflow")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `POST /change-sets/:changeSetId/return`                                        | returns it to its author, changes requested (`content.review`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `POST /change-sets/:changeSetId/approve`                                       | the review approval (`content.review`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `POST /change-sets/:changeSetId/sign-off`                                      | the sign-off restricted module types need (`content.approve`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `POST /change-sets/:changeSetId/merge`                                         | merges an approved change set into its base (`release.publish`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `POST /change-sets/:changeSetId/refresh`                                       | updates a draft from its base: merges the base's head into its branch (its author; see "Updating from the base")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `GET /change-sets/:changeSetId/refresh-conflicts`                              | the files an update reported conflicting, with when each was saved since (its author; paged; see "Updating from the base")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `GET /releases?filter[product]=`                                               | `Release`s, newest first: product revisions tagged on `main`, each with `meta.outputs` (`content.read`; see "Releases")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `GET /releases/:releaseId`                                                     | a `Release` (`content.read`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `POST /releases`                                                               | cuts a release: tags `main`'s commit as a product revision (`release.publish`; see "Releases")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `POST /releases/:releaseId/withdraw`                                           | withdraws a release, keeping its tag (`release.publish`, `{ meta: { reason } }`; see "Releases")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `GET /releases/:releaseId/changes`                                             | `ReleaseChange`s: the manuals and modules a release changed, per language (`content.read`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `GET /releases/:releaseId/outputs`                                             | `ReleaseOutput`s: a release's PDFs and their states (`content.read`; see "Releases")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `GET /releases/:releaseId/outputs/:outputId/file`                              | an output's file from the blob store (`content.read`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `POST /releases/:releaseId/outputs/:outputId/retry`                            | queues a failed output again (`release.publish`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `POST /releases/:releaseId/outputs/plan`                                       | plans a release's outputs again, adding only missing ones (`release.publish`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `GET /changelog?filter[product]=&filter[manual]=&filter[lang]=&filter[major]=` | `Release`s newest first with `meta.changedManuals` (`content.read`; see "Releases")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `GET /style-review`                                                            | a `StyleReviewStatus`: whether this server has a style review (`attributes.configured`), so the editor offers "Review style" only then (`content.edit`, #247)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `POST /change-sets/:changeSetId/modules/:lang/:moduleId/style-review`          | a `StyleReview`: a language model's style suggestions for one module, off by default (`content.edit`, its author only; see "Style review")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `GET /change-sets/:changeSetId/checks`                                         | `Check`s in what a change set changes, at its head (`content.read`; see "Checks")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `GET /change-sets/:changeSetId/review`                                         | where its review stands: required, pending, the approvals (`content.read.draft`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `GET /translations/status?filter[…]=`                                          | `TranslationStatus`es: each source module per target language, up to date, outdated, missing or at a provider (see "Translations")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `GET /translations/status/:lang/:moduleId`                                     | one `TranslationStatus`, block by block (see "Translations")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `POST /translations/exports`                                                   | a delta translation export: the changed blocks per target language as a `TranslationJob`, `meta.dryRun` to only count (`translation.manage`; see "Delta exports")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `GET /translations/exports`, `GET …/:jobId`                                    | `TranslationJob`s, newest first, the ones you may see (`translation.manage`; see "Delta exports")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `GET /translations/exports/:jobId/file?lang=`                                  | the job as XLIFF 2.0, or a ZIP of one per language (`translation.manage`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `POST /translations/exports/:jobId/cancel`                                     | cancels a sent job: its blocks are no longer at a provider (`translation.manage`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `GET /me/notifications`                                                        | `Notification`s: your own inbox, newest first, `filter[unread]`, `meta.unreadCount` (any actor; see "Notifications")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `GET /me/notifications/unread-count`                                           | your unread count for the bell, with an `ETag` (304 while unchanged)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `GET /me/notifications/:notificationId`                                        | one of your notifications (another person's is 404)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `POST /me/notifications/read`                                                  | marks yours read: `{ meta: { ids } }` or `{ meta: { all: true } }`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `GET /me/preferences`, `PATCH /me/preferences`                                 | your own preferences (#222): `showTips`, `dismissedTips` (at most 500 tip IDs; a change replaces the list), `tourDone`; the defaults until a first change; a service reads the defaults and changes none (403). Any person; never another's                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |

## Structure

Hexagonal, one folder per component (`project`, `modules` (with the module index), `manuals`, `variants`, `assets` (listing, serving and uploading images and attachments), `search`, `chunks` (retrieval chunks), `health`, `about` (the licence and the source), `branding` (the platform's name, logos and colours), `blobs`, `access`, `audit`, `change-sets` with its locks and working copies, `comments`, `checks`, `translations` (translation status, delta exports and imports), `outbox` (its consumers' cursors), `notifications`, `preferences` (each person's own: the editor's tips and tour), `releases`, `downloads` (the download centre, a read model over attachments and release outputs), `jobs` (background jobs), `style-review` (the model-based style review), `secrets` (the instance secret key), `webhooks` (subscribers and signed deliveries), `mail` (branded email through the installation's SMTP server), `storage-usage` (the storage an instance uses, and its optional limit), `backups` (scheduled, encrypted, verified backups), `sandbox` (the public sandbox mode)), plus `content` (the repository, shared), `database` (the Postgres connection, shared; see "Database") and `base`:

| Folder   | Contents                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| -------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `api/`   | controllers and JSON:API resources (Zod schemas that validate, document and test the responses)                                                                                                                                           |
| `core/`  | `actions/`: one use case each, with a `handle()` method and a unit test against an in-memory fake; ports as abstract classes (`ContentRepository`, `PdfRenderer`); domain errors                                                          |
| `repos/` | adapters implementing the ports: `GitContentRepo`, `TypstPdfRenderer`, `DrizzleDatabaseProbe`, `DrizzleUserDirectory`, `DrizzleServiceAccountStore`; a component's Drizzle tables (`<name>.tables.ts`)                                    |
| `base/`  | shared: JSON:API documents, negotiation and paging, the error filter, `@Public()` and `@CurrentActor()`, parameter validation, OpenAPI, domain errors, paging and the `SecretSealer` port in `base/core`, its AES adapter in `base/repos` |
| `tests/` | end-to-end tests against the full app over a sample repository, and the in-memory content fake                                                                                                                                            |

A lint rule keeps `core/` free of HTTP, Nest's HTTP exceptions, configuration, Git, the renderer and the database (`drizzle-orm`, `pg`, `src/database/`): it throws domain errors (`NotFoundError`, `InvalidContentError`, `UnavailableError`, …), which the API maps to statuses. Parameters are validated with Zod schemas (`@Param('id', { schema })`) by `SchemaValidationPipe`.

## Error codes

**Every error code has an entry in `error-codes.yaml`** (#420): its HTTP status and, in a sentence or two, when it happens. The docs site renders the file as its error code reference (`frontend/docs`). A change that raises a new code adds its entry in the same PR, in alphabetical order. A branch that merges main after another PR added codes keeps both sets of entries. `tests/error-codes.spec.ts` collects the codes from the source (`tests/error-codes.ts`) and fails in five cases: a domain error raised with a code it cannot read (see below), a code without an entry (its failure message prints the entry to paste, with the status the source gives and the detail it is raised with), an entry for a code the source no longer raises, a status the source answers with that the entry does not list, and an entry without `when` or out of order. A code is found where it is written as a literal in a non-spec file under `src/`:

- a `code = '…'` property of a `DomainError` subclass;
- the first argument of `new ConflictError('…', …)` and the other domain errors that take their code first;
- a `code: '…'` member of an object literal with a `detail` (the error objects the HTTP layer writes, an `UnprocessableProblems` problem, a job's or an output's error);
- the literal a local helper is called with as its `code` parameter (`refuse('token-revoked', …)`), the helper raising the error;
- a code forwarded from another error (`new UnprocessableError(error.code, …)`) under an `if` naming the codes it lets through: `error.code === '…'`, or `SET.has(error.code)` with `SET` a `new Set(['…'])` of the same file.

A domain error's code may also be either branch of a `? :`, a constant, or a constant object of literals indexed (`XLIFF_CODES[error.reason]`). A code computed any other way (`` `xliff-${reason}` ``) fails the test: write the codes as literals in one of these forms. A check's issues (`{ code, message, severity }`) are not error codes. A code reported elsewhere than in a response (a job's error, a warning in a report) has no `status`, and its `when` says where it shows.

## Database

Postgres through [Drizzle](https://orm.drizzle.team), with drizzle-kit migrations committed in `backend/api/migrations/`. Git stays the source of truth for content; Postgres holds operational state (users, roles, locks, comments, change sets, the audit log) and rebuildable state (the module index, see "Module index"; the release diffs, see "Releases"; the search index, see "Postgres search").

| Where                                        | What                                                                                                                                           |
| -------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `src/database/database.ts`                   | `Database`: the injectable handle (`db`, a Drizzle `PgDatabase`) with `ping()`, `migrate()` and `close()`                                      |
| `src/database/postgres.database.ts`          | the server's `Database`: a node-postgres pool on `CCMS_DATABASE_URL`                                                                           |
| `src/database/database.module.ts`            | `DatabaseModule` (global): connects and migrates before the server listens; closes after the HTTP server has stopped                           |
| `src/database/drizzle-unit-of-work.ts`       | `DrizzleUnitOfWork`, the `UnitOfWork` port (`base/core/unit-of-work.ts`): one Postgres transaction per `run`                                   |
| `src/database/schema.ts`                     | every table, re-exported from its component (`export * from '../<component>/repos/<name>.tables.js'`); drizzle-kit reads it                    |
| `src/database/migrate.main.ts`               | `pnpm nx run api:db-migrate`: applies pending migrations and exits                                                                             |
| `src/database/seed.main.ts`                  | `pnpm nx run api:db-seed -- --example` or `-- <file.json>`: roles and users (see "Users, roles and permissions")                               |
| `src/modules/index-rebuild.main.ts`          | `pnpm nx run api:index-rebuild`: drops the module index and the index of where files are used, and builds both again; empties the search index |
| `migrations/`, `drizzle.config.ts`           | what drizzle-kit generated (SQL, plus its snapshots and journal in `meta/`); never edited once merged                                          |
| `tests/database.ts`, `tests/global-setup.ts` | `createTestDatabase()`: a fresh in-memory PGlite database with every migration applied                                                         |

**Using it in a component.** A port in `core/` (an abstract class); an adapter in `repos/` that injects `Database` and queries `database.db`; the component's tables in `repos/<name>.tables.ts`, re-exported from `src/database/schema.ts`. `core/` never sees Drizzle (lint). Nothing to import: `DatabaseModule` is global.

**Writes that belong together** run in one transaction: an action injects `UnitOfWork` and calls `run(async (tx) => …)`, passing `tx` (an opaque `Transaction`) to every port that writes, e.g. `AuditLog.record(tx, …)`. It commits when the callback resolves and rolls everything back when it rejects. An adapter reaches the Drizzle transaction with `drizzleOf(tx)`, which refuses one whose `run` has returned. In unit tests, `FakeUnitOfWork` (`tests/fakes/`) applies what fakes staged only on success.

**A schema change.** Edit the tables, then `pnpm nx run api:db-generate -- --name <what>` (drizzle-kit writes `migrations/<n>_<what>.sql` and its snapshot); read the SQL and commit it all. One migration per issue; never edit a merged one. When the base branch gained a migration after you generated yours, merge the base, delete your unmerged migration (its `.sql`, its snapshot and its journal entry) and generate it again on top, so the chain stays linear. `pnpm nx run api:db-generate -- --custom --name <what>` gives an empty file for SQL drizzle-kit does not write (data changes, extensions).

**Applying migrations.** On start, before the server listens, unless `CCMS_DATABASE_MIGRATE=false` or the process is a worker (`CCMS_ROLE=worker`, #527: it never migrates, so an old worker never applies or a new one races a deploy; it waits up to 120 s until the newest migration of its own journal is applied, polling every 2 s, and stops start-up saying so past it; pg-boss's own schema it only checks, as the API installs it right after Drizzle's migrations, so outside compose, whose worker starts once the API is healthy, a worker started at the very same moment as an upgraded API may fail its first start and be restarted); or `pnpm nx run api:db-migrate` (in a deployment `node backend/api/dist/database/migrate.main.js`, which needs `backend/api/migrations/` next to `dist/`). Both hold a Postgres advisory lock while migrating (waiting at most 120 s for another process's migration; each statement then waits at most `CCMS_DATABASE_LOCK_TIMEOUT_MS` for a table lock, past which the attempt rolls back rather than stalling every query on that table: see "Migration lock policy" under Health), so servers starting together apply each migration once; that needs a direct connection or a session-mode pooler, not a transaction-mode one. Drizzle applies a migration only when its journal timestamp is later than the last one applied, so a test checks the journal is in order. Drizzle records what it applied in `drizzle.__drizzle_migrations`. A missing or malformed `CCMS_DATABASE_URL`, an unreachable server, refused credentials, a missing database or a failing migration stop start-up with a message saying which (never the password).

**Tests** run on [PGlite](https://pglite.dev), Postgres compiled to WebAssembly, in the test process: no Docker, no network, nothing shared between test files or agent slots. The global setup migrates one database per run; `createTestDatabase()` starts a copy of it (about 0.2 s), so call it per test file (`beforeAll`) or per test, and close it afterwards. `createTestApp()` gives the app its own (`t.database`); pass `{ database }` for another. The node-postgres adapter itself is tested against PGlite served on a loopback socket (`@electric-sql/pglite-socket`).

**Timing in tests.** `vitest.config.mts` gives every hook and test 30 s and runs at most a quarter of the cores as workers (each holds its own apps and databases, about 1 GB): on a machine running several checks at once a migrated app takes seconds to start, and the suite should slow down there, not fail. A test or file needing more says so itself. A spec's teardown skips what its `beforeAll` never made (`afterAll(() => t?.close())`), so a setup that timed out reports that one error; `createTestApp()` releases what it made when it fails. No test in the default suite asserts on wall-clock time.

**Speed criteria** (the module preview under 1 s, a search under 300 ms, a manual's PDF under 3 s cold and 1 s for another variant) are in `tests/perf/*.perf.ts`, run by `pnpm nx run api:perf` (`vitest.perf.config.mts`: one file at a time, one worker). `pnpm check` and `pnpm check:changed` run it after the other targets have finished, never from the cache, so every run measures afresh. Each measurement is printed (`perf: search, cold, median of 5: 39.6 ms (budget 300 ms; load …)`); `expectWithin()` asserts the budget only while the 1-minute load average is at most the core count, since above it a request waits for a CPU and the time measures the machine, and then says `busy, not asserted`.

**Many readers at once** (#479). One server process serves every request on one event loop, so what one request computes the others wait for. What makes reads under load cheap: a file is parsed once per blob, whichever commit or reader asks (`@openfolia/content-repo`, "What is kept in memory"; bounded by `CCMS_CONTENT_CACHE_MB`, and the indexes of the repository, blob IDs, changed files, histories and listings, by `CCMS_CONTENT_INDEX_CACHE_MB`, #552); a listing of every module reads 32 files at a time, so a single read never queues behind a whole listing in the one Git reader; requests asking for refs at once share one `for-each-ref` (never across a write of this process); the derived lists a request would build from every file are kept per commit and reading key, never shared between two readers who see differently: the search index (`CCMS_SEARCH_INDEXES`; a module's folded text is shared between indexes, per parsed module) and its rankings for paging, the manuals list, the asset usage, the translation reads; titles are compared with one collator (`compareTitles`), never `localeCompare` with options, which makes one per call. Heavy builds are bounded where they run (two search index builds at once); there is no 503 queue in front of reads, since a reader waits less for a shared build than for a retry.

**At scale**, outside `pnpm check`: `pnpm perf:seed --profile large` generates a large content repository, media and database (`.local/perf/`), and `pnpm nx run api:perf-scale -- --profile large` measures every list, read, PDF, import, XLIFF, MCP and concurrency case on it, as a Markdown and JSON report (`scripts/perf/README.md`, also for the editor's timings).
